- **doubt-driven-development**: Subjects every non-trivial decision to a fresh-context adversarial review before it stands. Use when a decision modifies branching logic, crosses a module or service boundary, asserts a property no compiler can check, depends on unseen context, has an irreversible blast radius, settles a prior-art verdict (Adopt/Extend/Compose/Build), or any time a confident output would be cheaper to verify now than to debug later. - **doubt-driven-zh**: 质疑驱动开发、对非平凡决策做冷上下文的对抗性复审、在提交前找漏洞。当用户说「帮我挑刺」「这段代码/方案哪里会出问题」「上线前多一双眼睛」「我不太确定这个改动安全」「让另一个模型审一下」「高风险迁移先审」，或改动涉及分支逻辑、跨模块边界、编译器验不了的属性（线程安全、幂等、顺序）、不可逆操作时使用。五步：CLAIM 写清主张与为何重要 → EXTRACT 剥出最小可审单元（产物 + 契约，不带你的推理）→ DOUBT 用"找问题、不要认可"的对抗提示交给冷上下文审稿者（只给产物与契约，不给主张；交互式必须提供跨模型二审的选项）→ RECONCILE 逐条按 契约误读/有效可行动/有效权衡/噪音 分类 → STOP 只剩琐碎、3 轮或用户拍板即停。改编自 addyosmani/agent-skills 的 doubt-driven-development（MIT）。 ([po-et/workbuddy-skills](https://github.com/po-et/workbuddy-skills/tree/HEAD/ported/skills/doubt-driven-zh))
- **doubt-escalation**: In-flight escalation (Doubt model) for the dowiz/DeliveryOS harness. When an OBSERVABLE signal fires mid-task (a loop of N failed attempts, a red-line/irreversible edit, ≥2 surviving interpretations, evidence conflict, novelty, or a result that contradicts a stated expectation), climb the cheapest-sufficient escalation rung within budget K — self-divergence → specialist/research subagent → stronger model → council → human — then RESUME the main task. Friction, not a hard stop, except irreversible/red-line which gate to a human. Routine reversible low-uncertainty work never escalates. - **dpd-edit**: Manual edit of a DPD node or pool item. Wraps close_node / add_node(provenance='manual') / pool_reject / pool unsuppress (= clear rejected_at). - **dpd-feedback**: Submit DPD dogfood feedback as a GitHub issue. User free-text description is the primary payload. Graph context is opt-in via manual copy-paste only. - **dpd-fill**: Generate inferred nodes for the current DPD graph: missing decompositions, unstated assumptions, gap candidates. Each inferred node requires user opt-in.
